930 entries, audit-analyst to Audytor WCAG. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **audit-analyst**: Read-only analyst for the security-audit graph. As a hunter it reviews the coverage units it is assigned in a source snapshot and reports each unit's disposition with the paths it read and the checks it made, plus any candidate finding; as a verifier it re-derives one candidate from the source without seeing anyone else's conclusion. It never edits files or runs anything, and it returns only the schema object it is asked for. ([gtlpanda/PandaStack](https://github.com/gtlpanda/PandaStack/blob/HEAD/graphs/security-audit/agents/audit-analyst.md) · ★1)

- **Auditor - Delta**: Compares two completed audit reports of the same product — a baseline snapshot and a current one — and produces a reconciled delta document classifying every finding as resolved, improved, unchanged, transformed, or unverified, plus a standalone open-items queue for remediation research. Findings with no baseline counterpart are marked provisional for a separate attribution agent to settle against the trees. Zwraca konkretne problemy z priorytetem i rekomendacja naprawy oraz rozroznia, co wykryl automat, a co wymaga testu ręcznego. Traktuje dostępność jak wymaganie, nie doklejke na koniec.
